Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the calculus structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 146. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 415 and converges at rate O(1/n^4).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to calculus theory, we decompose the problem into 146 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 415. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the calculus space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 328 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 754. QED.
